Outfit suggestion:

```
Hey there! That baby tee is a super fun find. Since it’s a fitted, cropped piece, it will balance out your looser bottoms perfectly. Here are two ways to style it:

**Option 1: Effortless Streetwear**
Pair the **Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print** with your **Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash**. Add the **Vintage black denim jacket** on top and finish with the **Chunky white sneakers**. The contrast between the fitted tee and baggy jeans is a classic Y2K silhouette.

**Option 2: Trendy Earthy Vibes**
Tuck the **Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print** into your **Wide-leg khaki trousers**. Use the **Brown leather belt** to define your waist and complete the look with your **Black combat boots**. It’s a great way to mix the cute butterfly print with a more grounded, minimal aesthetic.
```

Fit card:

```
Found the cutest Y2K baby tee with a butterfly print today! It was a total steal for just $18 and I’m so excited to get this listed on depop later.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This Y2K baby tee is a fun, versatile addition to your closet! Since the shirt is fitted and cropped, it will balance out your wider-cut bottoms perfectly. Here are two ways to style it:

**Option 1: The Y2K Streetwear Look**
*   **Top:** Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print
*   **Bottoms:** Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash
*   **Outerwear:** Black cropped zip hoodie (worn open)
*   **Shoes:** Chunky white sneakers
*   *Why it works:* The fitted tee contrasts beautifully with the baggy denim, and the cropped hoodie keeps the silhouette proportional.

**Option 2: The Soft-Cottagecore Vibe**
*   **Top:** Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print
*   **Bottoms:** Wide-leg khaki trousers
*   **Accessories:** Brown leather belt
*   **Shoes:** Black combat boots
*   *Why it works:* The butterfly print softens the professional feel of the khaki trousers, while the combat boots add a cool, grounded edge to the look.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This baby tee is a perfect addition to your wardrobe—it adds a fun pop of color that balances out your more neutral basics. Here are two ways to style it:

**Option 1: Y2K Streetwear**
Pair the **Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print** with your **Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash**. Add the **Black cropped zip hoodie** on top (worn open) and finish with the **Chunky white sneakers** and **Black crossbody bag** for a classic, effortless Y2K look.

**Option 2: Effortlessly Cool**
Style the **Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print** with your **Wide-leg khaki trousers**. Layer the **Vintage black denim jacket** over the top and pair with your **Black combat boots**. Use the **Brown leather belt** to accentuate the waist of the trousers for a trendy, "model-off-duty" aesthetic.
```

```
This butterfly baby tee is a perfect addition to your wardrobe! Because it’s fitted and cropped, it will balance out your existing bottoms beautifully. Here are two ways to style it:

**Option 1: Effortless Streetwear**
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
These vintage 501s are a great find—they offer a more structured, classic silhouette that balances out your existing baggy and wide-leg bottoms perfectly. 

Here are two ways to style them:

**1. The Classic Off-Duty Look**
*   **Top:** White ribbed tank top
*   **Outerwear:** Vintage black denim jacket
*   **Shoes:** Chunky white sneakers
*   **Accessory:** Brown leather belt
*   *Why it works:* This is a timeless, balanced look. Tucking in the tank and adding the belt highlights your waist, while the cropped jacket creates a clean, vintage-inspired silhouette.

**2. The Streetwear Edge**
*   **Top:** Black cropped zip hoodie
*   **Shoes:** Black combat boots
*   **Accessory:** Black crossbody bag
*   *Why it works:* The straight-leg cut of the 501s looks fantastic tucked into combat boots. The cropped hoodie keeps the proportions from feeling too heavy, giving you a sharp, monochromatic streetwear vibe.
```

Fit card:

```
Finally scored these perfect vintage Levi's 501s for just $38, and the medium wash is exactly the structured vibe I’ve been hunting for to balance out my closet. Grabbed them on depop and I’m already obsessed with how they fit.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
These Vintage Levi's 501s are a fantastic investment! Since they offer a more structured, classic fit compared to your baggy jeans, they will add great variety to your closet. 

Here are two easy ways to style them:

**1. The Classic Off-Duty Look**
Tuck your **White ribbed tank top** into the Levi's, layer on the **Vintage black denim jacket**, and finish with your **Chunky white sneakers**. Use the **Brown leather belt** to pull the waist together for a clean, timeless silhouette.

**2. The Streetwear Edge**
Pair the Levi's with your **Black cropped zip hoodie**. The cropped length of the hoodie will balance the straight-leg fit of the jeans perfectly. Complete the look with your **Black combat boots** and the **Black crossbody bag** for a cohesive, urban vibe.
```

Fit card:


- stopped early: no
- selected_item: Vintage Levi's 501 Jeans — Medium Wash ($38.0, depop)
- search_results: 4

Outfit suggestion:

```
These vintage 501s are a perfect addition because they offer a more structured, straight-leg fit compared to your baggy jeans. Here are two ways to style them:

**Outfit 1: The Classic Off-Duty Look**
*   **Top:** White ribbed tank top
*   **Outerwear:** Vintage black denim jacket
*   **Shoes:** Chunky white sneakers
*   **Accessory:** Brown leather belt
*   *Why it works:* Tucking the white tank into the 501s and adding the belt highlights your waist, while the black denim jacket creates a cool, high-contrast layered look.

**Outfit 2: The Edgy Streetwear Look**
*   **Top:** Black cropped zip hoodie
*   **Shoes:** Black combat boots
*   **Accessory:** Black crossbody bag
*   *Why it works:* The cropped length of the hoodie pairs perfectly with the higher rise of the 501s. The combat boots add a bit of grit that complements the "vintage" fading on the knees.
```

Fit card:

```
Finally scored the perfect pair of vintage Levi's 501s in that ideal medium wash for only $38. I'm obsessed with this structured fit and can't wait to style them with my combat boots; check my depop if you want to see how I’m pairing them this week!
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
These vintage 501s are a great score! They offer a slightly more structured, classic fit compared to your baggy jeans, making them perfect for balancing out your oversized pieces.

Here are two ways to style them:

**1. The "Off-Duty Cool" Look**
*   **Top:** White ribbed tank top (tucked in)
*   **Outerwear:** Vintage black denim jacket
*   **Shoes:** Chunky white sneakers
*   **Accessory:** Brown leather belt
*   *Why it works:* This is a classic denim-on-denim look. The fitted tank balances the structure of the 501s, while the brown belt adds a nice warm contrast to the indigo wash.

**2. The "Streetwear Edge" Look**
*   **Top:** Black cropped zip hoodie
*   **Shoes:** Black combat boots
*   **Accessory:** Black crossbody bag
*   *Why it works:* The cropped length of the hoodie highlights the waistline of the 501s, and the combat boots give the vintage denim a tough, grunge-inspired finish.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
These vintage 501s are a perfect addition to your closet because they offer a more structured, classic fit compared to your existing baggy jeans. Here are two ways to style them:

**1. The Off-Duty Classic**
Pair the **Vintage Levi's 501 Jeans** with the **White ribbed tank top** and the **Vintage black denim jacket**. Finish the look with the **Chunky white sneakers** and the **Black crossbody bag** for an easy, timeless streetwear vibe.

**2. The Edgy Contrast**
Tuck the **Oversized grey crewneck sweatshirt** into the **Vintage Levi's 501 Jeans** (use a "French tuck" to define your waist) and add the **Brown leather belt**. Pair with the **Black combat boots** to play up that vintage grunge aesthetic.
```

```
That butterfly baby tee is a perfect find! Since it has a fitted, cropped silhouette, it will balance out your wider bottoms beautifully. Here are two ways to style it:

**Option 1: Y2K Streetwear**
Pair the **Y2K Baby Tee** with your **Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash**. Add your **Black cropped zip hoodie** layered on top (left open) and finish with your **Chunky white sneakers** for a classic, nostalgic look.

**Option 2: Effortless Contrast**
Tuck the **Y2K Baby Tee** into your **Wide-leg khaki trousers**. Use the **Brown leather belt** to define your waist, and add the **Black combat boots** to give the outfit a bit of an edge. Layer the **Vintage black denim jacket** over your shoulders if you need an extra layer!
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This baby tee is a perfect addition to your wardrobe—it adds a fun pop of color and balances your more oversized pieces. Here are two ways to style it:

**Outfit 1: The Y2K Streetwear Look**
Pair the **Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print** with your **Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash**. Layer your **Black cropped zip hoodie** over the top (leaving it unzipped to show off the butterfly graphic) and finish with your **Chunky white sneakers**. 

**Outfit 2: The Soft Minimalist Look**
Tuck the **Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print** into your **Wide-leg khaki trousers**. Add your **Brown leather belt** to define the waist and complete the outfit with your **Black combat boots** for a cool, edgy contrast to the soft butterfly print.
```

Fit card:

```
Finally snagged this butterfly baby tee for just $18 and it’s giving such major Y2K energy. Can't wait to style this with baggy jeans or trousers for my new go-to look, so check out my depop if you want to see more of my thrifted finds!
```

```
This butterfly baby tee is a perfect addition to your wardrobe! Because it’s fitted and cropped, it will balance out the volume of your existing bottoms beautifully. Here are two ways to style it:

**Look 1: Y2K Streetwear**
Pair the **Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print** with your **Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash**. Add the **Black cropped zip hoodie** on top (worn open) and finish with your **Chunky white sneakers** for a classic early-2000s vibe.

**Look 2: Effortless Minimalist**
Tuck the **Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print** into your **Wide-leg khaki trousers**. Use the **Brown leather belt** to define your waist and pair it with your **Black combat boots** for a cool, grounded contrast to the soft butterfly graphic.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This flannel is a great find! It’s versatile enough to lean into your grunge aesthetic or balance out your more minimal pieces. Here are two ways to style it:

**Option 1: The Classic Grunge Look**
*   **Top:** White ribbed tank top
*   **Over-layer:** Oversized Flannel Shirt (worn open)
*   **Bottoms:** Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash
*   **Shoes:** Black combat boots
*   **Accessory:** Black crossbody bag

**Option 2: The Elevated Streetwear Look**
*   **Top:** Oversized Flannel Shirt (buttoned up and tucked in)
*   **Bottoms:** Wide-leg khaki trousers
*   **Accessory:** Brown leather belt
*   **Shoes:** Chunky white sneakers
*   **Layering tip:** If it gets chilly, layer the Black cropped zip hoodie underneath the flannel for a textured, streetwear vibe.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This is a great find! Since you already have some solid staples, here are two ways to style that flannel:

**1. The Classic Grunge Look**
Layer the **oversized flannel** unbuttoned over the **white ribbed tank top**. Pair it with your **baggy straight-leg jeans** (dark wash) and the **black combat boots**. This creates a perfectly balanced, effortless streetwear vibe.

**2. The Elevated Casual Look**
Tuck the **white ribbed tank top** into the **wide-leg khaki trousers** and wear the **oversized flannel** open on top. Use the **brown leather belt** to define your waist and finish the look with your **chunky white sneakers**. It’s comfortable, polished, and leans into those trendy earth tones.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This is a great find! Since the flannel is oversized, it’s perfect for layering. Here are two ways to style it:

**Option 1: The Classic Grunge Look**
Layer the **Oversized Flannel Shirt** (worn open) over the **White ribbed tank top**. Pair it with your **Baggy straight-leg jeans** and finish with the **Black combat boots**. It’s effortless, comfortable, and plays perfectly into that streetwear aesthetic.

**Option 2: The Elevated Casual Look**
Tuck the **White ribbed tank top** into the **Wide-leg khaki trousers**, then layer the **Oversized Flannel Shirt** over the top, tied at the waist or left unbuttoned. Add your **Chunky white sneakers** and the **Black crossbody bag** for a balanced, modern outfit that blends grunge with minimal earth tones.
```

Fit card:

```
Finally snagged this vintage Woolrich flannel for just $22 on thredUp and I’m obsessed with the oversized fit. It’s the perfect layering piece for that effortless grunge look I’ve been going for lately.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This is a fantastic, versatile find for your collection! Here are two ways to style that flannel:

**1. The 90s Grunge Look**
Layer the **oversized flannel shirt** (left open) over your **white ribbed tank top**. Pair it with your **baggy straight-leg jeans** and **black combat boots**. Finish with your **black crossbody bag** for a classic, effortless streetwear vibe.

**2. The Elevated Casual Look**
Tuck your **white ribbed tank top** into the **wide-leg khaki trousers** and leave the **oversized flannel shirt** unbuttoned as a lightweight "shacket" layer on top. Wear your **chunky white sneakers** and cinch the waist with the **brown leather belt** to add structure to the silhouette.
```


Outfit suggestion:

```
This red/black flannel is a fantastic, versatile addition to your wardrobe! Here are two ways to style it using your current pieces:

**Option 1: The Classic Grunge Look**
*   **Layering:** Wear the **White ribbed tank top** tucked into your **Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash**.
*   **The Flannel:** Wear the **Oversized flannel shirt** unbuttoned over the tank top.
*   **Finish:** Complete the look with your **Black combat boots** and the **Black crossbody bag**.

**Option 2: The Elevated Streetwear Look**
*   **Layering:** Wear the **White ribbed tank top** tucked into your **Wide-leg khaki trousers**.
*   **The Flannel:** Button the **Oversized flannel shirt** and tuck the front hem in, or wear it open as a light jacket. 
*   **Finish:** Add the **Brown leather belt** to define the waist and pair with your **Chunky white sneakers**.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This track jacket is a great score! It’s versatile enough to lean into the 90s aesthetic or keep things clean and minimal. Here are two ways to style it:

**Option 1: The Streetwear Look**
Pair the **90s Track Jacket** with your **White ribbed tank top** (worn underneath, partially unzipped) and the **Baggy straight-leg jeans**. Finish with the **Chunky white sneakers** and your **Black crossbody bag**. This plays up the vintage athletic vibe perfectly.

**Option 2: The Elevated Minimalist**
Style the **90s Track Jacket** over the **White ribbed tank top** tucked into your **Wide-leg khaki trousers**. Use the **Brown leather belt** to define your waist and wear the **Chunky white sneakers** to keep it relaxed and modern.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This 90s track jacket is a fantastic find—the navy and white combo is a timeless classic that will fit perfectly into your streetwear-leaning wardrobe. Here are two ways to style it:

**Option 1: The "Off-Duty Model" Look**
*   **Top:** White ribbed tank top
*   **Outerwear:** 90s Track Jacket — Navy/White Stripe (worn open)
*   **Bottoms:** Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash
*   **Shoes:** Chunky white sneakers
*   **Accessory:** Black crossbody bag
*   *Why it works:* This plays up the 90s aesthetic. Keep the jacket unzipped to show off the tank, and let the baggy denim balance out the streamlined fit of the track jacket.

**Option 2: The Elevated Sporty-Minimalist Look**
*   **Top:** White ribbed tank top
*   **Outerwear:** 90s Track Jacket — Navy/White Stripe (worn zipped up)
*   **Bottoms:** Wide-leg khaki trousers
*   **Shoes:** Chunky white sneakers
*   **Accessory:** Brown leather belt
*   *Why it works:* The navy and khaki color palette is incredibly sophisticated. Tucking the tank in and using the belt adds structure, making the track jacket feel more like a deliberate "fashion" piece rather than just gym wear.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This 90s track jacket is a great versatile find! Here are two effortless ways to style it using your current wardrobe:

**1. The Classic Streetwear Look**
*   **Top:** White ribbed tank top (under the track jacket)
*   **Bottom:** Baggy straight-leg jeans
*   **Shoes:** Chunky white sneakers
*   **Accessory:** Black crossbody bag
*   *Why it works:* This leans into the vintage athletic aesthetic. Leaving the jacket partially zipped or open over the tank creates a clean, intentional 90s silhouette.

**2. The "Model-Off-Duty" Mix**
*   **Top:** White ribbed tank top (under the track jacket)
*   **Bottom:** Wide-leg khaki trousers
*   **Shoes:** Black combat boots
*   **Accessory:** Brown leather belt
*   *Why it works:* The contrast between the sporty navy jacket and the structured khaki trousers creates a cool, elevated "high-low" vibe that feels very current.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This track jacket is a great score! It fits perfectly into your streetwear-leaning wardrobe. Here are two ways to style it:

**Option 1: Effortless 90s Streetwear**
*   **The Look:** Layer the **white ribbed tank top** under the open track jacket. Pair it with your **baggy straight-leg jeans** and the **chunky white sneakers**. 
*   **Why it works:** The navy and white palette is classic, and the silhouette plays with the retro, relaxed aesthetic of the 90s.

**Option 2: Elevated Minimalist**
*   **The Look:** Tuck the **white ribbed tank top** into your **wide-leg khaki trousers**. Add the **brown leather belt**, wear the track jacket zipped up halfway, and finish with your **chunky white sneakers**. 
*   **Why it works:** Mixing the athletic track jacket with tailored trousers creates a cool "high-low" contrast that feels intentional and modern.
```

Fit card:

```
Found this vintage Champion track jacket on Poshmark and I’m obsessed with the 90s athletic energy. It was a total steal for $45, and it’s already becoming my go-to piece for that perfect streetwear-meets-minimalist vibe.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This 90s track jacket is a great versatile find! Here are two ways to style it using your current wardrobe:

**1. The Classic Streetwear Look**
*   **Base:** White ribbed tank top
*   **Bottoms:** Baggy straight-leg jeans (dark wash)
*   **Outerwear:** 90s Track Jacket — Navy/White Stripe (worn unzipped)
*   **Shoes:** Chunky white sneakers
*   **Accessory:** Black crossbody bag

**2. The Cool-Girl Minimalist Look**
*   **Base:** White ribbed tank top (tucked in)
*   **Bottoms:** Wide-leg khaki trousers
*   **Outerwear:** 90s Track Jacket — Navy/White Stripe (zipped up as a top)
*   **Shoes:** Black combat boots
*   **Accessory:** Brown leather belt (to define the waist)
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This slip dress is a fantastic find! Because it’s silk and floral, it adds a beautiful, soft contrast to the edgier, structured pieces you already own.

Here are two ways to style it:

**1. The "90s Grunge" Look**
Layer your **oversized grey crewneck sweatshirt** over the dress (treating the dress like a skirt) and pair it with the **black combat boots**. This plays into the 90s aesthetic of the dress while keeping it cozy and effortless.

**2. The "Cool-Girl" Layered Look**
Wear the slip dress with your **vintage black denim jacket** on top and your **chunky white sneakers**. This balances the feminine floral print with some streetwear structure, making it perfect for a daytime outing.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This 90s silk slip dress is a fantastic versatile find! Because it’s lightweight, it acts as a perfect base layer for your existing pieces. Here are two ways to style it:

**1. The "90s Grunge" Look**
Layer your **Oversized grey crewneck sweatshirt** over the dress, letting the midi hem peek out at the bottom. Pair with your **Black combat boots** for that classic, effortless vintage aesthetic.

**2. The "Edgy Feminine" Look**
Wear the slip dress on its own and throw your **Vintage black denim jacket** over the top. Finish the outfit with your **Chunky white sneakers** and the **Black crossbody bag** for a balanced, everyday vibe that plays with textures.
```

```
This is a great find! A silk slip dress is incredibly versatile because it balances well with both feminine and edgy pieces. Here are two ways to style it using your current wardrobe:

**1. The "90s Grunge" Look**
Layer your **oversized grey crewneck sweatshirt** over the slip dress, letting the midi hem peek out at the bottom. Pair with your **black combat boots** for that classic effortless 90s aesthetic.

**2. The "Vintage Street" Look**
Wear the slip dress as a base layer and throw your **vintage black denim jacket** over the top. Finish the outfit with your **chunky white sneakers** to keep the vibe cool, casual, and comfortable for daytime wear.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
These Vintage Levi's 501s are a fantastic addition—they’ll give you that classic, structured fit that balances out your more oversized pieces. Here are two ways to style them:

**1. The Classic Everyday Look**
Tuck your **White ribbed tank top** into the jeans and layer the **Vintage black denim jacket** on top. Finish with the **Brown leather belt**, **Black crossbody bag**, and **Chunky white sneakers**. This is a timeless, effortless outfit perfect for running errands.

**2. The Edgy Streetwear Look**
Pair the 501s with the **Black cropped zip hoodie**. Add the **Black combat boots** to lean into the vintage-grunge vibe. This works well because the straight leg of the 501s creates a great silhouette against the cropped hoodie and boots.
```

```
Finally scored the perfect pair of vintage Levi's 501s for just $38. They have that exact lived-in, structured wash I’ve been hunting for, and I’m so stoked to list them on depop!
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
These vintage 501s are a fantastic addition! Because they offer a more structured, classic fit compared to your existing baggy jeans, they’ll add great versatility to your closet. 

Here are two easy ways to style them:

**Outfit 1: Effortless Off-Duty**
*   **Top:** White ribbed tank top (tucked in)
*   **Bottom:** Vintage Levi's 501 Jeans
*   **Outerwear:** Vintage black denim jacket
*   **Shoes:** Chunky white sneakers
*   **Accessory:** Brown leather belt
*   *Why it works: A classic denim-on-denim look that plays with textures and fits.*

**Outfit 2: Streetwear Edge**
*   **Top:** Black cropped zip hoodie
*   **Bottom:** Vintage Levi's 501 Jeans
*   **Shoes:** Black combat boots
*   **Accessory:** Black crossbody bag
*   *Why it works: The cropped hoodie balances the higher waist of the 501s, creating a sharp, modern silhouette.*
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
These vintage Levi’s 501s are a fantastic addition; they provide a more structured, classic silhouette that balances out your existing collection of baggy and oversized pieces. Here are two ways to style them:

**1. The Classic Off-Duty Look**
Tuck your **white ribbed tank top** into the Levi’s and layer the **vintage black denim jacket** on top. Finish with the **chunky white sneakers** and the **black crossbody bag**. The straight-leg 501s will look great paired with the cropped jacket to create a clean, balanced silhouette.

**2. The Streetwear Grunge Look**
Pair the Levi’s with your **black cropped zip hoodie** and **black combat boots**. Use the **brown leather belt** to define the waist. The slightly faded medium wash of the jeans will pop against the black pieces, creating a cool, textured contrast that leans into your streetwear aesthetic.
```

Fit card:

```
Finally scored the perfect pair of vintage Levi’s 501s in this ideal medium wash to balance out all my baggy fits. Snagged these for just $38 and I’m already obsessing over how they look on my depop feed.
```

- stopped early: no
- selected_item: Vintage Levi's 501 Jeans — Medium Wash ($38.0, depop)
- search_results: 4

Outfit suggestion:

```
These vintage 501s are a fantastic addition! They offer a slightly more structured fit compared to your baggy jeans, giving you more styling versatility. Here are two ways to style them:

**1. The Classic Off-Duty Look**
*   **Top:** White ribbed tank top
*   **Outerwear:** Vintage black denim jacket
*   **Shoes:** Chunky white sneakers
*   **Accessories:** Brown leather belt
*   *Why it works:* This is a timeless, balanced silhouette. Tucking the white tank into the 501s and cinching with the brown belt keeps the look polished, while the black denim jacket adds a bit of edge.

**2. The Streetwear Contrast**
*   **Top:** Oversized grey crewneck sweatshirt
*   **Shoes:** Black combat boots
*   **Accessories:** Black crossbody bag
*   *Why it works:* The "slim-straight" cut of the 501s balances out the volume of your oversized sweatshirt. Tucking the front of the sweatshirt into the waistband helps define your shape, and the combat boots ground the vintage denim perfectly.
```

Fit card:

```
Finally secured the perfect pair of vintage Levi’s 501s for just $38! Honestly obsessed with how this medium wash fits, so catch me living in these from now on. Listed these beauties over on depop for anyone else hunting for that classic structured denim look.
```

```
These vintage 501s are a great investment—they provide a "straight-leg" silhouette that balances your existing wide-leg and baggy denim perfectly. Here are two ways to style them:

**Outfit 1: The Classic Everyday**
Pair the **Vintage Levi's 501 Jeans** with your **White ribbed tank top** and the **Vintage black denim jacket**. Complete the look with your **Chunky white sneakers** and the **Brown leather belt**. This gives you a clean, timeless vibe that works for almost any casual outing.

**Outfit 2: Edgy Streetwear**
Style the **Vintage Levi's 501 Jeans** with the **Black cropped zip hoodie**. Since the jeans are a classic fit and the hoodie is cropped, this combination is incredibly flattering. Finish it off with your **Black combat boots** and **Black crossbody bag** for a sharp, cohesive look.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
That Y2K baby tee is a perfect find! Because it’s fitted and cropped, it will balance out your wider-leg bottoms beautifully. Here are two ways to style it:

**Option 1: The "Y2K Streetwear" Look**
Pair the **Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print** with your **Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash**. Throw on the **Black cropped zip hoodie** (worn open) and your **Chunky white sneakers**. This plays into the fitted-top-meets-baggy-bottom silhouette that defines the Y2K aesthetic.

**Option 2: The "Cottagecore Minimalist" Look**
Tuck the **Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print** into your **Wide-leg khaki trousers**. Add the **Brown leather belt** to cinch the waist and finish with your **Black combat boots**. This creates a nice contrast between the soft butterfly print and the structured earth tones of your trousers.
```


Outfit suggestion:

```
Here are two great ways to style that Y2K baby tee with your current wardrobe:

**Outfit 1: The Y2K Streetwear Look**
Pair the **Y2K Baby Tee** with your **Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash**. Add the **Black cropped zip hoodie** layered over top (leave it unzipped to show off the butterfly print) and finish with your **Chunky white sneakers** for a perfect early-2000s vibe.

**Outfit 2: The Soft-Minimalist Look**
Tuck the **Y2K Baby Tee** into your **Wide-leg khaki trousers**. Cinch the waist with the **Brown leather belt** and layer the **Vintage black denim jacket** on top. Complete the look with your **Black combat boots** to give the "cottagecore" print a cool, edgy contrast.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This Y2K baby tee is a fun, versatile addition to your wardrobe! Here are two easy ways to style it:

**Outfit 1: The Y2K Streetwear Look**
Pair the **Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print** with your **Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash** and **Chunky white sneakers**. The fitted crop of the tee perfectly balances the volume of the baggy jeans. Add your **Black crossbody bag** to complete the look.

**Outfit 2: The Soft-Minimalist Look**
Tuck the **Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print** into your **Wide-leg khaki trousers**. Use your **Brown leather belt** to define the waist, and wear your **Black combat boots** to give the outfit a bit of an edge. Layer your **Vintage black denim jacket** on top if you need an extra layer.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This Y2K baby tee is a perfect addition to your wardrobe! Because it’s fitted and cropped, it will balance out your looser bottoms beautifully. Here are two ways to style it:

**Option 1: The "Y2K Streetwear" Look**
Pair the **Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print** with your **Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash**. Add the **Black cropped zip hoodie** layered over top (leaving it unzipped to show off the print) and finish with your **Chunky white sneakers** for a classic early-2000s vibe.

**Option 2: The "Soft-Cottagecore" Look**
Tuck the **Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print** into your **Wide-leg khaki trousers**. Use the **Brown leather belt** to define your waist, throw on the **Vintage black denim jacket**, and complete the outfit with your **Black combat boots** to ground the look.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This Y2K baby tee is a perfect addition to your closet! Because it’s fitted and cropped, it will create a great silhouette when paired with your wider bottoms. Here are two ways to style it:

**Option 1: The Y2K Streetwear Look**
*   **Top:** Y2K Baby Tee
*   **Bottoms:** Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash
*   **Outerwear:** Vintage black denim jacket
*   **Shoes:** Chunky white sneakers
*   **Accessory:** Black crossbody bag
*   *Why it works:* This plays up the Y2K aesthetic while balancing the fitted tee with the baggy denim.

**Option 2: The "Cottagecore-Minimal" Mix**
*   **Top:** Y2K Baby Tee
*   **Bottoms:** Wide-leg khaki trousers
*   **Shoes:** Black combat boots
*   **Accessory:** Brown leather belt
*   *Why it works:* The butterfly print adds a feminine touch to the structured khaki trousers, and the boots add a cool, grounded edge to the outfit.
```

```
Finally found the butterfly baby tee of my dreams and honestly, it’s the perfect fit for that 2000s energy. I’ve got it listed on depop for just $18 if you want to snag it before I change my mind and keep it!
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This flannel is a fantastic find! Since it’s oversized, you want to balance the volume to keep your silhouette looking intentional. Here are two ways to style it:

**Option 1: The Classic Grunge Look**
Layer the **Oversized Flannel Shirt** open over the **White ribbed tank top**. Pair it with your **Baggy straight-leg jeans** and the **Black combat boots**. This creates that effortless, layered streetwear vibe that’s perfect for casual days.

**Option 2: Elevated Contrast**
Tuck the **White ribbed tank top** into the **Wide-leg khaki trousers** and wear the **Oversized Flannel Shirt** unbuttoned over the top (or tie it around your waist). Finish with your **Chunky white sneakers** and the **Black crossbody bag** for a clean, put-together look that balances the earth tones with the classic red plaid.
```


Outfit suggestion:

```
This is a fantastic, versatile addition to your closet! Since it's already oversized, it will pair perfectly with your existing streetwear staples. Here are two ways to style it:

**Option 1: The Classic Grunge Look**
*   **Top:** White ribbed tank top (tucked in)
*   **Layer:** Oversized flannel shirt (worn open)
*   **Bottoms:** Baggy straight-leg jeans
*   **Shoes:** Black combat boots
*   **Accessories:** Black crossbody bag

**Option 2: Effortless Minimalist**
*   **Top:** White ribbed tank top (tucked in)
*   **Bottoms:** Wide-leg khaki trousers
*   **Layer:** Oversized flannel shirt (worn buttoned or tied at the waist)
*   **Shoes:** Chunky white sneakers
*   **Accessories:** Brown leather belt

**Styling Tip:** Since the flannel is oversized, try tucking just the front hem into your jeans or trousers to give your waist a little definition!
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This is a fantastic, versatile addition to your wardrobe! Here are two ways to style that flannel:

**1. The 90s Grunge Look**
Layer the **white ribbed tank top** tucked into your **baggy straight-leg jeans**. Wear the flannel unbuttoned over the top and finish the look with your **black combat boots** and the **black crossbody bag**. 

**2. Elevated Casual**
Tuck the **white ribbed tank top** into the **wide-leg khaki trousers** and secure them with the **brown leather belt**. Wear the flannel buttoned up or tied around your waist, and style with your **chunky white sneakers** for a clean, streetwear-inspired vibe.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This flannel is a fantastic find! Given your existing wardrobe, here are two ways to style it:

**Option 1: The Classic Grunge Look**
*   **Top:** Oversized Flannel Shirt (open) over your White ribbed tank top
*   **Bottom:** Baggy straight-leg jeans
*   **Shoes:** Black combat boots
*   **Accessory:** Black crossbody bag
*   *Why it works:* This leans into the streetwear/grunge aesthetic. Keeping the tank tucked in creates a nice silhouette under the oversized flannel.

**Option 2: Elevated Street-Minimalist**
*   **Top:** Oversized Flannel Shirt (buttoned up and tucked)
*   **Bottom:** Wide-leg khaki trousers
*   **Shoes:** Chunky white sneakers
*   **Accessory:** Brown leather belt
*   *Why it works:* The tan trousers soften the red/black plaid, making the outfit feel a bit more pulled together while staying comfortable and practical.
```

Fit card:

```
Finally scored this vintage Woolrich flannel and it’s giving such perfect 90s grunge energy. I can’t believe I snagged it for only $22 on thredUp!
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This oversized flannel is a perfect find for your existing wardrobe. Here are two ways to style it:

**1. The 90s Grunge Look**
Layer the **oversized flannel shirt** open over the **white ribbed tank top**. Pair it with your **baggy straight-leg jeans** and finish with the **black combat boots** and **black crossbody bag**. This balances the volume of the flannel with a fitted base.

**2. The Elevated Streetwear Look**
Wear the **oversized flannel shirt** buttoned up and tucked into the **wide-leg khaki trousers**. Use the **brown leather belt** to define your waist, and pair with the **chunky white sneakers**. It’s a clean, intentional take on the oversized aesthetic.
```

Fit card:


Outfit suggestion:

```
This 90s track jacket is a great versatile find! Here are two ways to style it using your current wardrobe:

**Look 1: Effortless Streetwear**
Layer the **90s Track Jacket** over the **white ribbed tank top** and pair it with your **baggy straight-leg jeans**. Finish with the **chunky white sneakers** and your **black crossbody bag** for a clean, cohesive 90s-inspired vibe.

**Look 2: Elevated Contrast**
Style the **90s Track Jacket** with the **wide-leg khaki trousers**. Keep the jacket zipped halfway to create a relaxed, balanced silhouette, and pair it with the **black combat boots** and the **brown leather belt** to bridge the gap between athletic and minimal styles.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This is a great find! Since the jacket is lightweight and has that classic 90s aesthetic, it will pair perfectly with your current rotation. Here are two ways to style it:

**Option 1: The Off-Duty Streetwear Look**
*   **Jacket:** 90s Track Jacket — Navy/White Stripe
*   **Top:** White ribbed tank top
*   **Bottom:** Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash
*   **Shoes:** Chunky white sneakers
*   **Accessory:** Black crossbody bag
*   *Why it works:* This leans into the 90s vibe. Tuck the tank in, keep the jacket unzipped, and let the white sneakers tie in the stripe detail on the sleeves.

**Option 2: The Elevated Minimalist Look**
*   **Jacket:** 90s Track Jacket — Navy/White Stripe
*   **Top:** White ribbed tank top
*   **Bottom:** Wide-leg khaki trousers
*   **Shoes:** Black combat boots
*   **Accessory:** Brown leather belt
*   *Why it works:* Mixing the sporty track jacket with tailored trousers creates a sophisticated "high-low" contrast. The brown belt adds a nice touch of warmth to the navy and tan color palette.
```

Fit card:

```
Obsessed with this vintage Champion track jacket I just scored; the navy and white stripes are giving major 90s retro energy. I can't believe I snagged it for only $45 on Poshmark!
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This is a great find! A 90s track jacket is a versatile staple that works perfectly with the items you already own. Here are two ways to style it:

**Option 1: The Streetwear Look**
Pair the **90s Track Jacket** with your **white ribbed tank top** and **baggy straight-leg jeans**. Finish it off with your **chunky white sneakers** and the **black crossbody bag**. The track jacket adds a sporty, retro vibe to your favorite denim.

**Option 2: The Minimalist Contrast**
Layer the **90s Track Jacket** over your **white ribbed tank top** and pair it with the **wide-leg khaki trousers**. Add your **chunky white sneakers** to keep it fresh and clean. The navy-and-tan color combination looks polished, intentional, and very "off-duty model."
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This is a great find! Since the jacket is lightweight and has that classic 90s aesthetic, it will pair perfectly with the pieces you already own. Here are two ways to style it:

**Outfit 1: The Streetwear Classic**
*   **Top:** White ribbed tank top
*   **Outerwear:** 90s Track Jacket — Navy/White Stripe
*   **Bottoms:** Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash
*   **Shoes:** Chunky white sneakers
*   **Accessory:** Black crossbody bag
*   *Why it works:* This leans into the 90s athletic vibe. The white tank keeps the look clean underneath, and the chunky sneakers tie in the white stripe on the sleeves.

**Outfit 2: Elevated Casual**
*   **Top:** White ribbed tank top
*   **Outerwear:** 90s Track Jacket — Navy/White Stripe
*   **Bottoms:** Wide-leg khaki trousers
*   **Shoes:** Black combat boots
*   **Accessory:** Brown leather belt
*   *Why it works:* Mixing the sporty track jacket with tailored trousers creates a cool "high-low" contrast. The combat boots add a bit of edge to the navy and khaki color palette.
```

```
This track jacket is a great find! Since it's lightweight and hits that 90s streetwear vibe, here are two effortless ways to style it:

**Option 1: The Classic 90s Streetwear Look**
*   **Top:** White ribbed tank top (tucked in)
*   **Bottom:** Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash
*   **Outerwear:** 90s Track Jacket — Navy/White Stripe (worn unzipped)
*   **Shoes:** Chunky white sneakers

**Option 2: The Modern Minimalist Mix**
*   **Top:** White ribbed tank top
*   **Bottom:** Wide-leg khaki trousers
*   **Outerwear:** 90s Track Jacket — Navy/White Stripe (zipped up)
*   **Shoes:** Chunky white sneakers
*   **Accessory:** Brown leather belt (to define the waist)
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This slip dress is a fantastic find! Because it’s silk, it will add a great textural contrast to the structured and casual pieces you already own. Here are two ways to style it:

**1. The "90s Grunge" Look**
Layer the **oversized grey crewneck sweatshirt** over the slip dress, letting the midi hem peek out at the bottom. Pair this with your **black combat boots** and the **black crossbody bag** for an effortless, edgy vibe that plays perfectly into the 90s aesthetic.

**2. The "Layered Chic" Look**
Wear the slip dress on its own and layer the **vintage black denim jacket** over the top. Finish the outfit with your **chunky white sneakers** for a comfortable, feminine-meets-streetwear look that is perfect for weekend outings.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This is a fantastic find! Slip dresses are incredibly versatile because they layer so well. Here are two ways to style it using your current wardrobe:

**1. The "90s Grunge" Look**
Layer your **oversized grey crewneck sweatshirt** over the slip dress, letting the midi hem peek out at the bottom. Pair with your **black combat boots** for that effortless, moody vintage aesthetic.

**2. The "Edgy Feminine" Look**
Wear the slip dress on its own with your **vintage black denim jacket** draped over your shoulders. Finish the look with your **chunky white sneakers** for a comfortable, cool outfit that balances the delicate silk with streetwear staples.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This 90s silk slip dress is a fantastic versatile find! It pairs perfectly with the items you already have to create some great contrast. Here are two ways to style it:

**1. The "90s Grunge" Look**
Layer the **oversized grey crewneck sweatshirt** over the dress, letting the midi hem peek out underneath. Pair with the **black combat boots** and the **black crossbody bag**. This balances the delicate silk with a cozy, effortless silhouette.

**2. The "Edgy Feminine" Look**
Wear the slip dress on its own and throw the **vintage black denim jacket** over your shoulders. Finish the outfit with the **chunky white sneakers** for a modern, street-style vibe that makes the floral print feel grounded and cool.
```

```
That slip dress is a fantastic find! It’s versatile enough to transition from a delicate feminine look to an edgy 90s grunge vibe. Here are two ways to style it using your current wardrobe:

**1. The "90s Grunge" Look**
Layer your **oversized grey crewneck sweatshirt** over the slip dress, letting the floral hem peek out at the bottom. Pair with your **black combat boots** and the **black crossbody bag**. The contrast between the soft silk and the chunky, oversized knit is a classic 90s silhouette.

**2. The "Cool-Girl Layered" Look**
Wear the slip dress with your **vintage black denim jacket** draped over your shoulders. Finish the outfit with your **chunky white sneakers** for a comfortable, everyday vibe that balances the femininity of the floral print.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This Y2K baby tee is a fun, versatile addition to your wardrobe! Here are two easy ways to style it:

**Outfit 1: The Y2K Streetwear Look**
*   **Top:** Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print
*   **Bottom:** Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash
*   **Outerwear:** Black cropped zip hoodie
*   **Shoes:** Chunky white sneakers
*   **Accessory:** Black crossbody bag
*   *Why it works:* The fitted tee balances the volume of your baggy jeans, and the cropped hoodie keeps the silhouette sharp and trendy.

**Outfit 2: The Soft-Cottagecore Minimalist**
*   **Top:** Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print
*   **Bottom:** Wide-leg khaki trousers
*   **Outerwear:** Vintage black denim jacket
*   **Shoes:** Black combat boots
*   **Accessory:** Brown leather belt
*   *Why it works:* Tucking the tee into your khakis with the brown belt highlights your waist, while the black denim jacket and boots add an edgy, "vintage-cool" finish to the butterfly print.
```

```
This Y2K baby tee is a fun, versatile addition to your wardrobe! Here are two easy ways to style it:

**Option 1: The Trendy Streetwear Look**
Pair the **Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print** with your **Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash**. Add the **Black cropped zip hoodie** on top (worn open) and finish with your **Chunky white sneakers**. The contrast between the fitted tee and baggy denim perfectly captures that Y2K aesthetic.

**Option 2: The Soft Minimalist Look**
Tuck the **Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print** into your **Wide-leg khaki trousers**. Use the **Brown leather belt** to define your waist and complete the outfit with your **Black combat boots**. It’s a great way to balance the playful butterfly print with structured, earth-toned pieces.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
That Y2K baby tee is a perfect addition to your closet! Since it has a fitted, cropped silhouette, it will balance out your existing bottoms beautifully. Here are two ways to style it:

**Option 1: The Y2K Streetwear Look**
Pair the **Y2K Baby Tee** with your **Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash**. Add your **Chunky white sneakers** and the **Black crossbody bag**. The contrast between the tight tee and the baggy denim is a classic, flattering silhouette.

**Option 2: Effortless Minimalist**
Tuck the **Y2K Baby Tee** into your **Wide-leg khaki trousers**. Layer the **Vintage black denim jacket** on top for a bit of edge, and finish the look with your **Black combat boots** to ground the softer colors of the tee.
```
